# Launcher error-message audit

Review inventory, 2026-09-27. IDs in the first column are references for follow-up decisions. Recommendations are **not** authorization or implementation. Implemented scope includes the original status/clipboard fixes and the explicitly requested final presentation polish described below.

This is a source-backed inventory of display entry points, their callers, literal messages, dynamic families, and caught/non-displayed failures that would otherwise be easy to mistake for visible errors. It is not an enumeration of every possible operating-system or dependency string. `${…}` marks interpolation (the source expression is retained where its value is important); `\n` means a displayed newline. `Error:` is JavaScript's `String(Error)` prefix; Python errors cross the bridge as `str(exception)` and receive that prefix when JavaScript wraps them. Perl may append ` at -e line ${line}.` and a newline. Equivalent translations are grouped by English key.

Pre-status-fix evidence is normal-history commit `727ff2ca310b608caa1c3d2407597d2e342cbc3a` (also the parent of local verification snapshot `31d819a6046746447e8910af1c7fe9e905c3d5c5`); use `git show 727ff2c:PATH` for pre-patch source. This audit baseline is distinct from the upstream fork baseline `514ebed`. Line links below target the committed source. The before/after rows deliberately preserve misleading pre-fix behavior. Fixtures and build-tool errors are excluded from product messages; runtime helpers and the bundled Sophon service are included.

Final polish, 2026-09-28: the selected review items are now implemented. Historical wording remains in “This patch.” Current UI text uses the operation message without the JavaScript `Error:` prefix. Operation-boundary wrappers retain original stack/cause, command output, helper JSON and native objects in `neutralinojs.log`; logs-only message definitions below remain diagnostic evidence, not newly visible errors. The exact English running text is `game is running (DO NOT CLOSE THE LAUNCHER)` (previously `Game is running. DO NOT CLOSE THE LAUNCHER!`). Successful game cleanup and startup recovery now leave the idle status blank. Recommendations outside this focused pass remain for human review.

### Display paths examined

- **Red launcher alert:** `launchOwnership.state().failed` previously selected the same mutable `detail` used for progress. `problem()` latched `failed`, and `phase()` then replaced `detail`. Now the alert reads `error`; phases cannot write it. Reservation clears prior session error/warning state.
- **Launcher status and queues:** primary and predownload queues consume `setStateText`. `LaunchFailure` is caught and shown as queue status; other exceptions go through the native fatal dialog and guarded shutdown. The ownership status overrides queued status while held. Running state is pinned to the exact requested English sentence only after attributed confirmation in the FPS bridge; older non-FPS clients still yield their pre-existing `GAME_RUNNING` key before their command completes (see coverage limits).
- **Native dialogs:** `fatal`, localized `alert`/`prompt`, startup fallback and bootstrap panel. `fatal` now renders `errorMessage(error)` and records the original diagnostic, then waits for shutdown and requests exit -1. Raw String/JSON rendering was historical behavior. If ownership cannot be released, the shutdown can remain waiting after the dialog is acknowledged.
- **Inline settings:** FPS target validation and FPS persistence error. The sole `notificationService.show` is informational advanced-settings availability, not an error toast.
- **Recovery/diagnostics:** startup restoration, Launch Fix, retained private runtimes, registry/file journals, bridge/job observations. A diagnostic or exception is not automatically a user error; local catches and automatic retries are noted below.
- **No universal post-startup rejection handler:** several settings callbacks and manual update checks are async without an explicit user-facing catch. These are inventoried as unresolved presentation paths, rather than incorrectly claiming a dialog appears.

## 1a. Same-wrapper scenarios with materially different outcomes

These rows expand dynamic families where the same exception wrapper can otherwise obscure what actually failed.

| ID | Displayed text | Trigger | Actual outcome | UI location | User action | Source evidence | Recommendation | This patch |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| FPS-TIMEOUT-01 | <code>Error: Game discovery timed out</code> | No attributed identity before 90-second discovery budget, or one discover RPC exceeds its remaining/10-second operation deadline. | FPS discovery failed. Does not prove the launch failed or succeeded; transaction still independently observes the issued request. Waiting-for-game status remains unless validated running evidence arrived. | Before: UI-003 red Launch failed. After: UI-012 warning plus diagnostic log. | Keep launcher open; inspect launch log if no game window appears. | [src/clients/mhy/hk4e/fps-companion.ts:149](../src/clients/mhy/hk4e/fps-companion.ts#L149); [src/clients/mhy/hk4e/fps-companion.ts:213](../src/clients/mhy/hk4e/fps-companion.ts#L213) → [src/clients/mhy/hk4e/launch-transaction.ts:68](../src/clients/mhy/hk4e/launch-transaction.ts#L68) | Move to feature warning; raw timeout name in logs. | Routing changed, deadlines and native lifetime retention unchanged. |
| FPS-TIMEOUT-02 | <code>Error: Game probe timed out</code> | A retained identity isAlive call does not settle within 10 seconds, including time queued behind other bridge requests. | FPS observation stops; prior validated game can be running and playable. Late probe is still awaited; ownership and independent game lifetime remain. | Before: UI-003 red Launch failed. After: UI-012 warning plus diagnostic log. | Continue playing if game is usable; keep launcher open. FPS may be unavailable. | [src/clients/mhy/hk4e/fps-companion.ts:149](../src/clients/mhy/hk4e/fps-companion.ts#L149); [src/clients/mhy/hk4e/fps-companion.ts:213](../src/clients/mhy/hk4e/fps-companion.ts#L213) → [src/clients/mhy/hk4e/launch-transaction.ts:68](../src/clients/mhy/hk4e/launch-transaction.ts#L68) | Move to feature warning; raw timeout name in logs. | Routing changed, deadlines and native lifetime retention unchanged. |

## 4. Backend/native helper message families

Python backend strings pass through the threaded task error/status bridge (or online-info response) to JavaScript. Individual OS, TLS, curl, decompressor, JSON/protobuf and filesystem exception texts are open-ended; the literal guards and wrappers are listed. Perl rows include the command/supervisor outer wrapper rather than claiming raw stderr is shown standalone.

| ID | Displayed text | Trigger | Actual outcome | UI location | User action | Source evidence | Recommendation | This patch |

A partly committed update is not certified complete until final target verification. | Native fatal dialog as <code>Error: Update cancelled; verified work is retained for restart</code> (backend task error), or startup panel for metadata | No action required for intended cancellation; restart operation when ready. | [sophon_server/full_update.py:41](../sophon_server/full_update.py#L41) → [sophon_server/utils.py:97](../sophon_server/utils.py#L97) → [src/sophon.ts:149](../src/sophon.ts#L149) → [src/launcher/task-queue.ts:31](../src/launcher/task-queue.ts#L31) → [src/utils/neu.ts:230](../src/utils/neu.ts#L230) | Move to non-error warning/status for deliberate cancellation. | Unchanged; recommendation only. |

## 6. Pre-fix status leakage and bridge bookkeeping detail

All phase messages below could replace a red error after failed latched true. Some bridge diagnostic reports also called problem directly. These are not new product errors; retained here for human review of the pre-fix behavior.

## Most misleading or unactionable messages for review

Current disposition after final polish (committed as `29ce22b`). Historical wording in the inventory is evidence, not an outstanding-work list.

### Resolved presentation issues

- **UI-003/UI-005:** optional probe failures now produce an FPS warning; progress cannot overwrite a red error. Genuine launch/lifetime failures remain errors.
- **UI-004/EXT-010:** recovered cleanup-only history no longer leaves a final failure. Confirmed successful cleanup clears status; genuine primary failures remain visible.
- **TS-008 / EXT-004:** the Aria2 admission FIXME is replaced with destination/state context. Download control-flow gaps remain deferred below.
- **UI-017:** observation uses `Check game status again`; restoration uses `Retry safe cleanup`. Their existing guards are unchanged.
- **UI-013/UI-026:** unknown exit status is an explicit warning, distinct from a confirmed abnormal exit.
- **Command/registry wrappers, PL rows, NATIVE-009:** selected operation boundaries now explain the failed action; raw command/helper evidence stays in diagnostics. Technical Launch Fix wording (UI-018) and other unwrapped native/helper errors remain review items.
- **UI-023/UI-027:** failed admission stops saying “Preparing launch”; successful recovery and game cleanup leave blank idle status. Intentional startup cancellation uses a closing/cleanup heading.

### Still deferred

1. **LOC incremental-update rows:** YES/NO version prompts still ignore the answer; review behavior before changing message text.
2. **EXT-004:** download streaming still lacks complete terminal-state handling and can poll without delay for zero-length totals.
3. **EXT-007/EXT-008:** some async settings callbacks and legacy launch paths still lack dependable visible failure handling.
4. **EXT-011:** artwork loading/decoding can still block startup.
5. **EXT-009:** a post-update/download storage-flag failure can still look like failure of the main operation.
6. **TS startup wrappers / EXT-003 / UI-022:** service wrappers may obscure specific causes; `exec2` stream capture is incomplete; postlaunch diagnostic loss may remain logs-only. Preserve evidence while improving these paths.

## Coverage and limitations

The audit began at every identified launcher-owned visible failure surface (red ownership panel, queue status exception branch, native fatal/localized dialogs, bootstrap native/root fallback, inline FPS validation/save errors, warning panel, and notification service). It traces backward through all production TypeScript Error/LaunchFailure constructors and rejection/catch sites, English translation keys with active alerts/prompts, every Python raise/assert/abortlog/job-error producer in the bundled Sophon source, runtime Perl die/error assignments, and native bootstrap/bridge response families. Installation, launcher/game updates, predownloads, archive extraction, verification/repair, configuration persistence, Wine acquisition/setup, game launch, optional FPS/Steam/Launch Fix/HDR/window/fullscreen/Game Mode, ownership/recovery and cleanup are covered.

Supported source channels reviewed: HK4E global/China (including legacy selection paths), BH3 global, HSR global/China, NAP global/China, and CBJQ global/China. Equivalent channel translations share key rows. Commented-out unsupported-version alerts in HK4E/HSR/NAP are dormant, not claimed live. Build/developer-only fixture errors are excluded. `Size not match`, `Md5 not match`, intermediate Sophon HTTP/parse exceptions, timeout sentinels and legacy diff-fallback events are explicitly identified as caught/transformed rather than falsely presented as dialogs.

This is a comprehensive source inventory, **not a claim that every row was exercised live**. Unresolved paths: arbitrary OS/network/native/dependency exception text; source branches whose async UI callbacks have no catch; legacy NAP backend routes exposed by the service but not used by the current NAP frontend's Aria2 update path; native OS permission/crash dialogs; errors before the native API connects; indefinite acknowledgement/cleanup waits; whether every historical service branch remains reachable under current remote manifests. Raw native/library code tables are represented as dynamic families, not copied as an invented complete list of possible messages. The generic wrapper rows intentionally cover additional I/O exceptions from call sites that contain no literal Error constructor.

The exact-delay cause of the user's original probe timeout cannot be proved without that run's logs. Code establishes that the 10-second deadline includes a serialized mailbox request/response and transport latency while the game lifetime is independently held; it is not a game-exit timeout. The regression reproduces a delayed probe with the attributed game still alive. No timeout was increased, no unrelated process was accepted as success, and no game termination or ownership-release shortcut was added.

Verification results and the packaged app path are recorded in `docs/launcher-status-clipboard-verification.md`. Native clipboard fixture results are distinguished there from tests of production resources and from actual game execution.


## Final-polish follow-ups intentionally deferred

- Ignored prompt responses in existing repair/update paths.
- Aria2/download polling state handling; TS-008 wording is improved, its control flow is unchanged.
- Missing error catches in some asynchronous settings callbacks.
- Startup dependence on remote artwork loading/decoding.

The final polish changes presentation and recovered-history classification, not these behaviors. The earlier coverage limits still apply. See [final build and installation verification](launcher-final-polish-verification.md) for what was actually executed.
